Title: Relation d'un voyage du Levant, fait par ordre du Roy, contenant l'histoire ancienne et moderne de plusieurs isles de l'Archipel, de Constantinople, des côtes de la Mer Noire, de l'Arménie, de la Géorgie, des frontières de Perse et de l'Asie Mineure; Tome troisième
Creator: Joseph Pitton de Tournefort
Publisher: Lyon: chez Anisson et Posuel, 1717, avec privilège du Roi
Publication Date: 1717
Rights: Public Domain (published 1717, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item gri_relationdunv00tour (https://archive.org/details/gri_relationdunv00tour), the item's own gri_relationdunv00tour_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Getty Research Institute; host metadata date: 1717. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: e2d07ffe12fda1f5229dd0991faf8f42dea8449c30e2893c0a1e7aae196d20dc.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Getty Research Institute scan of the Lyon edition in three volumes; this is the third. Title page legible; the body is readable with the long s kept.
Content note: The French original of the letters on the journey through Erzurum, Armenia and Georgia, vendored beside the English translation.
